+
+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t clampCoordinate(const long long coordinate,
+                                                  const size_t extent) {
+    if (coordinate < 0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (coordinate >= static_cast<long long>(extent)) {
+        return extent - 1;
+    }
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(const double concentration,
+                                                    const double laplacian) {
+    // Kept in the same form as the original free-energy derivative.
+    return 4.5 * ((concentration + 1.0) * kEAA + (concentration - 1.0) * kEBB -
+                  2.0 * concentration * kEAB) +
+           3.0 * concentration + concentration * concentration * concentration -
+           kGamma * laplacian;
 }
 
+// A fused, shared-memory implementation of one complete Cahn-Hilliard step.
+// It preserves the original two-stencil formulation while avoiding global
+// stores and reloads of mu.  All threads participate in the tile construction,
+// including boundary threads whose output falls outside a partial final block.
+__global__ void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ current,
+                                 double* __restrict__ next, const size_t nx,
+                                 const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    __shared__ double concentrationTile[kCTileSize];
+    __shared__ double muTile[kMuTileSize];
+
+    const int threadLinear =
+        (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) * kBlockY + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y)) * kBlockX +
+        static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Load a two-cell clamped halo.  The one-dimensional cooperative mapping
+    // has coalesced accesses within each x row and keeps the load balanced.
+    for (int load = threadLinear; load < kCTileSize; load += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int tileX = load % kCTileX;
+        const int tileY = (load / kCTileX) % kCTileY;
+        const int tileZ = load / kCTileXY;
+        const long long rawX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + tileX - kHalo;
+        const long long rawY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + tileY - kHalo;
+        const long long rawZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + tileZ - kHalo;
+        const size_t x = clampCoordinate(rawX, nx);
+        const size_t y = clampCoordinate(rawY, ny);
+        const size_t z = clampCoordinate(rawZ, nz);
+        concentrationTile[load] = current[z * plane + y * nx + x];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Form mu for the output block plus a one-cell halo.
+    for (int item = threadLinear; item < kMuTileSize; item += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int muX = item % kMuTileX;
+        const int muY = (item / kMuTileX) % kMuTileY;
+        const int muZ = item / kMuTileXY;
+        const long long baseX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;
+        const long long baseY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;
+        const long long baseZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;
+        // Clamp the mu coordinate before selecting its concentration center.
+        // Evaluating mu at a virtual clamped concentration coordinate would
+        // use a different stencil than the physical boundary cell.
+        const int centerX = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseX + muX - 1, nx)) -
+                                              (baseX - kHalo));
+        const int centerY = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseY + muY - 1, ny)) -
+                                              (baseY - kHalo));
+        const int centerZ = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseZ + muZ - 1, nz)) -
+                                              (baseZ - kHalo));
+        const int center = centerZ * kCTileXY + centerY * kCTileX + centerX;
+        const double value = concentrationTile[center];
+        const double cxx = concentrationTile[center + 1] + concentrationTile[center - 1] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double cyy = concentrationTile[center + kCTileX] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileX] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double czz = concentrationTile[center + kCTileXY] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        muTile[item] = chemicalPotential(value, cxx + cyy + czz);
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x < nx && y < ny && z < nz) {
+        const int muCenter = (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + 1) * kMuTileXY +
+                             (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + 1) * kMuTileX +
+                             static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + 1;
+        const double muValue = muTile[muCenter];
+        const double mxx = muTile[muCenter + 1] + muTile[muCenter - 1] - 2.0 * muValue;
+        const double myy = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileX] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileX] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const double mzz = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileXY] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const int concentrationCenter =
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + kHalo) * kCTileXY +
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + kHalo) * kCTileX +
+            static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + kHalo;
+        next[z * plane + y * nx + x] =
+            concentrationTile[concentrationCenter] + kTimeStepDiffusivity * (mxx + myy + mzz);
+    }
+}

+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                      \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,          \
+                         cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                     \
+            return EXIT_FAILURE;                                                              \
+        }                                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+

+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t clampCoordinate(const long long coordinate,
+                                                  const size_t extent) {
+    if (coordinate < 0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (coordinate >= static_cast<long long>(extent)) {
+        return extent - 1;
+    }
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(const double concentration,
+                                                    const double laplacian) {
+    // Kept in the same form as the original free-energy derivative.
+    return 4.5 * ((concentration + 1.0) * kEAA + (concentration - 1.0) * kEBB -
+                  2.0 * concentration * kEAB) +
+           3.0 * concentration + concentration * concentration * concentration -
+           kGamma * laplacian;
 }
 
+// A fused, shared-memory implementation of one complete Cahn-Hilliard step.
+// It preserves the original two-stencil formulation while avoiding global
+// stores and reloads of mu.  All threads participate in the tile construction,
+// including boundary threads whose output falls outside a partial final block.
+__global__ void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ current,
+                                 double* __restrict__ next, const size_t nx,
+                                 const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    __shared__ double concentrationTile[kCTileSize];
+    __shared__ double muTile[kMuTileSize];
+
+    const int threadLinear =
+        (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) * kBlockY + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y)) * kBlockX +
+        static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Load a two-cell clamped halo.  The one-dimensional cooperative mapping
+    // has coalesced accesses within each x row and keeps the load balanced.
+    for (int load = threadLinear; load < kCTileSize; load += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int tileX = load % kCTileX;
+        const int tileY = (load / kCTileX) % kCTileY;
+        const int tileZ = load / kCTileXY;
+        const long long rawX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + tileX - kHalo;
+        const long long rawY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + tileY - kHalo;
+        const long long rawZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + tileZ - kHalo;
+        const size_t x = clampCoordinate(rawX, nx);
+        const size_t y = clampCoordinate(rawY, ny);
+        const size_t z = clampCoordinate(rawZ, nz);
+        concentrationTile[load] = current[z * plane + y * nx + x];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Form mu for the output block plus a one-cell halo.
+    for (int item = threadLinear; item < kMuTileSize; item += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int muX = item % kMuTileX;
+        const int muY = (item / kMuTileX) % kMuTileY;
+        const int muZ = item / kMuTileXY;
+        const long long baseX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;
+        const long long baseY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;
+        const long long baseZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;
+        // Clamp the mu coordinate before selecting its concentration center.
+        // Evaluating mu at a virtual clamped concentration coordinate would
+        // use a different stencil than the physical boundary cell.
+        const int centerX = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseX + muX - 1, nx)) -
+                                              (baseX - kHalo));
+        const int centerY = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseY + muY - 1, ny)) -
+                                              (baseY - kHalo));
+        const int centerZ = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseZ + muZ - 1, nz)) -
+                                              (baseZ - kHalo));
+        const int center = centerZ * kCTileXY + centerY * kCTileX + centerX;
+        const double value = concentrationTile[center];
+        const double cxx = concentrationTile[center + 1] + concentrationTile[center - 1] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double cyy = concentrationTile[center + kCTileX] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileX] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double czz = concentrationTile[center + kCTileXY] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        muTile[item] = chemicalPotential(value, cxx + cyy + czz);
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x < nx && y < ny && z < nz) {
+        const int muCenter = (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + 1) * kMuTileXY +
+                             (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + 1) * kMuTileX +
+                             static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + 1;
+        const double muValue = muTile[muCenter];
+        const double mxx = muTile[muCenter + 1] + muTile[muCenter - 1] - 2.0 * muValue;
+        const double myy = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileX] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileX] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const double mzz = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileXY] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const int concentrationCenter =
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + kHalo) * kCTileXY +
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + kHalo) * kCTileX +
+            static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + kHalo;
+        next[z * plane + y * nx + x] =
+            concentrationTile[concentrationCenter] + kTimeStepDiffusivity * (mxx + myy + mzz);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \
+    do {                                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                      \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,          \
+                         cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                     \
+            return EXIT_FAILURE;                                                              \
+        }                                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+} // namespace

codex
The corrected kernel now agrees with the CPU reference to floating-point roundoff for regular, irregular, and degenerate grids. I also checked strict no-FMA mode; it did not make the hash bit-identical across CPU/GPU, but reduced throughput substantially, so the optimized IEEE double-precision CUDA path remains enabled.

+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t clampCoordinate(const long long coordinate,
+                                                  const size_t extent) {
+    if (coordinate < 0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (coordinate >= static_cast<long long>(extent)) {
+        return extent - 1;
+    }
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(const double concentration,
+                                                    const double laplacian) {
+    // Kept in the same form as the original free-energy derivative.
+    return 4.5 * ((concentration + 1.0) * kEAA + (concentration - 1.0) * kEBB -
+                  2.0 * concentration * kEAB) +
+           3.0 * concentration + concentration * concentration * concentration -
+           kGamma * laplacian;
 }
 
+// A fused, shared-memory implementation of one complete Cahn-Hilliard step.
+// It preserves the original two-stencil formulation while avoiding global
+// stores and reloads of mu.  All threads participate in the tile construction,
+// including boundary threads whose output falls outside a partial final block.
+__global__ void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ current,
+                                 double* __restrict__ next, const size_t nx,
+                                 const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    __shared__ double concentrationTile[kCTileSize];
+    __shared__ double muTile[kMuTileSize];
+
+    const int threadLinear =
+        (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) * kBlockY + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y)) * kBlockX +
+        static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Load a two-cell clamped halo.  The one-dimensional cooperative mapping
+    // has coalesced accesses within each x row and keeps the load balanced.
+    for (int load = threadLinear; load < kCTileSize; load += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int tileX = load % kCTileX;
+        const int tileY = (load / kCTileX) % kCTileY;
+        const int tileZ = load / kCTileXY;
+        const long long rawX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + tileX - kHalo;
+        const long long rawY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + tileY - kHalo;
+        const long long rawZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + tileZ - kHalo;
+        const size_t x = clampCoordinate(rawX, nx);
+        const size_t y = clampCoordinate(rawY, ny);
+        const size_t z = clampCoordinate(rawZ, nz);
+        concentrationTile[load] = current[z * plane + y * nx + x];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Form mu for the output block plus a one-cell halo.
+    for (int item = threadLinear; item < kMuTileSize; item += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int muX = item % kMuTileX;
+        const int muY = (item / kMuTileX) % kMuTileY;
+        const int muZ = item / kMuTileXY;
+        const long long baseX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;
+        const long long baseY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;
+        const long long baseZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;
+        // Clamp the mu coordinate before selecting its concentration center.
+        // Evaluating mu at a virtual clamped concentration coordinate would
+        // use a different stencil than the physical boundary cell.
+        const int centerX = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseX + muX - 1, nx)) -
+                                              (baseX - kHalo));
+        const int centerY = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseY + muY - 1, ny)) -
+                                              (baseY - kHalo));
+        const int centerZ = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseZ + muZ - 1, nz)) -
+                                              (baseZ - kHalo));
+        const int center = centerZ * kCTileXY + centerY * kCTileX + centerX;
+        const double value = concentrationTile[center];
+        const double cxx = concentrationTile[center + 1] + concentrationTile[center - 1] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double cyy = concentrationTile[center + kCTileX] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileX] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double czz = concentrationTile[center + kCTileXY] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        muTile[item] = chemicalPotential(value, cxx + cyy + czz);
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x < nx && y < ny && z < nz) {
+        const int muCenter = (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + 1) * kMuTileXY +
+                             (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + 1) * kMuTileX +
+                             static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + 1;
+        const double muValue = muTile[muCenter];
+        const double mxx = muTile[muCenter + 1] + muTile[muCenter - 1] - 2.0 * muValue;
+        const double myy = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileX] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileX] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const double mzz = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileXY] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const int concentrationCenter =
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + kHalo) * kCTileXY +
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + kHalo) * kCTileX +
+            static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + kHalo;
+        next[z * plane + y * nx + x] =
+            concentrationTile[concentrationCenter] + kTimeStepDiffusivity * (mxx + myy + mzz);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \
+    do {                                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                      \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,          \
+                         cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                     \
+            return EXIT_FAILURE;                                                              \
+        }                                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+} // namespace
+

   213	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   214	    size_t nx = 64;
   215	    size_t ny = 0;
   216	    size_t nz = 0;
   217	    int iterations = 20;
   218	    bool validate = false;
   219	    bool printResults = false;
   220	
   221	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   222	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   223	            nx = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   224	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   225	            ny = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   226	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   227	            nz = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   228	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   229	            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   230	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   231	            validate = true;
   232	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   233	            printResults = true;
   234	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   235	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   236	            return EXIT_SUCCESS;
   237	        } else {
   238	            std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   239	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   240	            return EXIT_FAILURE;
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    if (ny == 0) {
   245	        ny = nx;
   246	    }
   247	    if (nz == 0) {
   248	        nz = nx;
   249	    }
   250	    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
   251	        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz) {
   252	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Grid dimensions must form a non-empty addressable volume\n");
   253	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
   257	    const size_t bytes = gridSize * sizeof(double);
   258	    const size_t blocksX = (nx + kBlockX - 1) / kBlockX;
   259	    const size_t blocksY = (ny + kBlockY - 1) / kBlockY;
   260	    const size_t blocksZ = (nz + kBlockZ - 1) / kBlockZ;
   261	    if (blocksX > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max() ||
   262	        blocksY > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max() ||
   263	        blocksZ > std::numeric_limits<unsigned int>::max()) {
   264	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Grid dimensions exceed CUDA launch limits\n");
   265	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   266	    }
   267	
   268	    std::printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
   269	    std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   270	    std::printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   271	    std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   272	
   273	    std::vector<double> concentration(gridSize);
   274	    std::printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   275	    initializeConcentration(concentration, nx, ny, nz);
   276	
   277	    double* deviceCurrent = nullptr;
   278	    double* deviceNext = nullptr;
   279	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
   280	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceCurrent, bytes));
   281	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&deviceNext, bytes));
   282	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(deviceCurrent, concentration.data(), bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   283	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetCacheConfig(cahnHilliardStep, cudaFuncCachePreferShared));
   284	
   285	    const dim3 block(kBlockX, kBlockY, kBlockZ);
   286	    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>(blocksX), static_cast<unsigned int>(blocksY),
   287	                    static_cast<unsigned int>(blocksZ));
   288	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
   289	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
   290	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   291	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   292	
   293	    std::printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   294	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
   295	    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   296	        cahnHilliardStep<<<grid, block>>>(deviceCurrent, deviceNext, nx, ny, nz);
   297	        std::swap(deviceCurrent, deviceNext);
   298	    }
   299	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
   300	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   301	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   302	
   303	    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   304	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
   305	    std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", elapsedMilliseconds);
   306	
   307	    const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations;
   308	    const double mcups = cellUpdates / (static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / 1000.0) / 1.0e6;
   309	    std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   310	
   311	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(concentration.data(), deviceCurrent, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   312	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   313	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   314	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceCurrent));
   315	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(deviceNext));
   316	
   317	    if (printResults) {
   318	        print_results(concentration, "Concentration");
   319	    }
   320	
   321	    if (validate) {
   322	        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   323	        if (validateResult(concentration, nx, ny, nz)) {
   324	            std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   325	            return EXIT_SUCCESS;
   326	        }
   327	        std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   328	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   329	    }
   330	
   331	    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
   332	}

+
+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t clampCoordinate(const long long coordinate,
+                                                  const size_t extent) {
+    if (coordinate < 0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (coordinate >= static_cast<long long>(extent)) {
+        return extent - 1;
+    }
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(const double concentration,
+                                                    const double laplacian) {
+    // Kept in the same form as the original free-energy derivative.
+    return 4.5 * ((concentration + 1.0) * kEAA + (concentration - 1.0) * kEBB -
+                  2.0 * concentration * kEAB) +
+           3.0 * concentration + concentration * concentration * concentration -
+           kGamma * laplacian;
 }
 
+// A fused, shared-memory implementation of one complete Cahn-Hilliard step.
+// It preserves the original two-stencil formulation while avoiding global
+// stores and reloads of mu.  All threads participate in the tile construction,
+// including boundary threads whose output falls outside a partial final block.
+__global__ void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ current,
+                                 double* __restrict__ next, const size_t nx,
+                                 const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    __shared__ double concentrationTile[kCTileSize];
+    __shared__ double muTile[kMuTileSize];
+
+    const int threadLinear =
+        (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) * kBlockY + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y)) * kBlockX +
+        static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Load a two-cell clamped halo.  The one-dimensional cooperative mapping
+    // has coalesced accesses within each x row and keeps the load balanced.
+    for (int load = threadLinear; load < kCTileSize; load += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int tileX = load % kCTileX;
+        const int tileY = (load / kCTileX) % kCTileY;
+        const int tileZ = load / kCTileXY;
+        const long long rawX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + tileX - kHalo;
+        const long long rawY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + tileY - kHalo;
+        const long long rawZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + tileZ - kHalo;
+        const size_t x = clampCoordinate(rawX, nx);
+        const size_t y = clampCoordinate(rawY, ny);
+        const size_t z = clampCoordinate(rawZ, nz);
+        concentrationTile[load] = current[z * plane + y * nx + x];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Form mu for the output block plus a one-cell halo.
+    for (int item = threadLinear; item < kMuTileSize; item += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int muX = item % kMuTileX;
+        const int muY = (item / kMuTileX) % kMuTileY;
+        const int muZ = item / kMuTileXY;
+        const long long baseX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;
+        const long long baseY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;
+        const long long baseZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;
+        // Clamp the mu coordinate before selecting its concentration center.
+        // Evaluating mu at a virtual clamped concentration coordinate would
+        // use a different stencil than the physical boundary cell.
+        const int centerX = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseX + muX - 1, nx)) -
+                                              (baseX - kHalo));
+        const int centerY = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseY + muY - 1, ny)) -
+                                              (baseY - kHalo));
+        const int centerZ = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseZ + muZ - 1, nz)) -
+                                              (baseZ - kHalo));
+        const int center = centerZ * kCTileXY + centerY * kCTileX + centerX;
+        const double value = concentrationTile[center];
+        const double cxx = concentrationTile[center + 1] + concentrationTile[center - 1] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double cyy = concentrationTile[center + kCTileX] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileX] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double czz = concentrationTile[center + kCTileXY] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        muTile[item] = chemicalPotential(value, cxx + cyy + czz);
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x < nx && y < ny && z < nz) {
+        const int muCenter = (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + 1) * kMuTileXY +
+                             (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + 1) * kMuTileX +
+                             static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + 1;
+        const double muValue = muTile[muCenter];
+        const double mxx = muTile[muCenter + 1] + muTile[muCenter - 1] - 2.0 * muValue;
+        const double myy = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileX] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileX] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const double mzz = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileXY] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const int concentrationCenter =

+__device__ __forceinline__ size_t clampCoordinate(const long long coordinate,
+                                                  const size_t extent) {
+    if (coordinate < 0) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (coordinate >= static_cast<long long>(extent)) {
+        return extent - 1;
+    }
+    return static_cast<size_t>(coordinate);
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(const double concentration,
+                                                    const double laplacian) {
+    // Kept in the same form as the original free-energy derivative.
+    return 4.5 * ((concentration + 1.0) * kEAA + (concentration - 1.0) * kEBB -
+                  2.0 * concentration * kEAB) +
+           3.0 * concentration + concentration * concentration * concentration -
+           kGamma * laplacian;
 }
 
+// A fused, shared-memory implementation of one complete Cahn-Hilliard step.
+// It preserves the original two-stencil formulation while avoiding global
+// stores and reloads of mu.  All threads participate in the tile construction,
+// including boundary threads whose output falls outside a partial final block.
+__global__ void cahnHilliardStep(const double* __restrict__ current,
+                                 double* __restrict__ next, const size_t nx,
+                                 const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    __shared__ double concentrationTile[kCTileSize];
+    __shared__ double muTile[kMuTileSize];
+
+    const int threadLinear =
+        (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) * kBlockY + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y)) * kBlockX +
+        static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+
+    // Load a two-cell clamped halo.  The one-dimensional cooperative mapping
+    // has coalesced accesses within each x row and keeps the load balanced.
+    for (int load = threadLinear; load < kCTileSize; load += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int tileX = load % kCTileX;
+        const int tileY = (load / kCTileX) % kCTileY;
+        const int tileZ = load / kCTileXY;
+        const long long rawX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + tileX - kHalo;
+        const long long rawY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + tileY - kHalo;
+        const long long rawZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + tileZ - kHalo;
+        const size_t x = clampCoordinate(rawX, nx);
+        const size_t y = clampCoordinate(rawY, ny);
+        const size_t z = clampCoordinate(rawZ, nz);
+        concentrationTile[load] = current[z * plane + y * nx + x];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    // Form mu for the output block plus a one-cell halo.
+    for (int item = threadLinear; item < kMuTileSize; item += kBlockThreads) {
+        const int muX = item % kMuTileX;
+        const int muY = (item / kMuTileX) % kMuTileY;
+        const int muZ = item / kMuTileXY;
+        const long long baseX = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX;
+        const long long baseY = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY;
+        const long long baseZ = static_cast<long long>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ;
+        // Clamp the mu coordinate before selecting its concentration center.
+        // Evaluating mu at a virtual clamped concentration coordinate would
+        // use a different stencil than the physical boundary cell.
+        const int centerX = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseX + muX - 1, nx)) -
+                                              (baseX - kHalo));
+        const int centerY = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseY + muY - 1, ny)) -
+                                              (baseY - kHalo));
+        const int centerZ = static_cast<int>(static_cast<long long>(
+                                                  clampCoordinate(baseZ + muZ - 1, nz)) -
+                                              (baseZ - kHalo));
+        const int center = centerZ * kCTileXY + centerY * kCTileX + centerX;
+        const double value = concentrationTile[center];
+        const double cxx = concentrationTile[center + 1] + concentrationTile[center - 1] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double cyy = concentrationTile[center + kCTileX] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileX] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        const double czz = concentrationTile[center + kCTileXY] +
+                           concentrationTile[center - kCTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * value;
+        muTile[item] = chemicalPotential(value, cxx + cyy + czz);
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockX + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockY + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * kBlockZ + threadIdx.z;
+    if (x < nx && y < ny && z < nz) {
+        const int muCenter = (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + 1) * kMuTileXY +
+                             (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + 1) * kMuTileX +
+                             static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + 1;
+        const double muValue = muTile[muCenter];
+        const double mxx = muTile[muCenter + 1] + muTile[muCenter - 1] - 2.0 * muValue;
+        const double myy = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileX] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileX] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const double mzz = muTile[muCenter + kMuTileXY] + muTile[muCenter - kMuTileXY] -
+                           2.0 * muValue;
+        const int concentrationCenter =
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.z) + kHalo) * kCTileXY +
+            (static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) + kHalo) * kCTileX +
+            static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) + kHalo;
+        next[z * plane + y * nx + x] =
+            concentrationTile[concentrationCenter] + kTimeStepDiffusivity * (mxx + myy + mzz);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \
+    do {                                                                                      \
+        const cudaError_t cudaStatus = (call);                                                \
+        if (cudaStatus != cudaSuccess) {                                                      \
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,          \
+                         cudaGetErrorString(cudaStatus));                                     \
+            return EXIT_FAILURE;                                                              \
+        }                                                                                     \
+    } while (false)
+
+} // namespace
